# REFS_NOTES — companion to `spec/refs_merged.bib`

Generated 2026-10-10 by the spec synthesizer. **Writers cite only keys that appear in `refs_merged.bib`**, and attribute to a source **only** what is documented here or in `research/survey/SURVEY.md` (with its [txt]/[abs]/[bib] marker). If you need a statement from a source that is not documented, mark it `(verify)` in your report instead of writing it as fact.

## 0. Provenance, counts, how to use

| part | origin | entries | verification |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `research/refs/refs.bib` (refs agent; readable keys `<Surname…><Year>`) | 203 | INSPIRE + Crossref + arXiv API + DOI handles (2026-10-09) |
| 2 | `research/survey/survey_refs.bib` minus duplicates of part 1 (INSPIRE texkeys) | 260 | INSPIRE by journal ref/arXiv; 2 manual entries via Crossref (2026-10-09) |
| 3 | SUPPLEMENT: references verified by derivation agents or required by the final outline | 172 | re-fetched from INSPIRE 2026-10-10, cross-checked first author, journal, volume, first page, year (one hit per query) |
| 4 | classical mathematics | 10 | Crossref (DOIs) / Open Library (books) 2026-10-10 |
| | **total** | **645** | `bibtex` with `unsrt`: 0 warnings; XeLaTeX with `common/preamble_fact.tex` + `\nocite{*}`: 0 errors, 0 overfull boxes, 0 missing glyphs (37 pp.) |

**Deduplication.** 155 survey records duplicated refs entries (match by INSPIRE texkey, DOI or arXiv number); the refs key was kept. `Collins:2011zzd` (INSPIRE texkey shared by the 2011 book and its 2023 OA reissue) maps to `Collins2011`; cite `Collins2011` for page/equation numbers (pagination of `Collins2023` not compared). `Zimmermann:1970Brandeis` → `Zimmermann1970`. In the supplement, `BreitenlohnerMaison1977b` = `Breitenlohner:1975hg` (CMP 52, 39) and `BreitenlohnerMaison1977c` = `Breitenlohner:1976te` (CMP 52, 55).

**Aliases.** `spec/key_aliases.tsv` (alias → merged key → origin) maps every survey texkey/label and every key used in an agent's LaTeX snippet to the merged key. Before reusing text from `research/*/` snippets or from `research/survey/master_table_zh.tex`, replace aliases, e.g. with

```python
# python3 -I replace_aliases.py SNIPPET.tex > SNIPPET_fixed.tex
import re, sys
al = {}
for ln in open('spec/key_aliases.tsv', encoding='utf-8'):
    a, k = ln.rstrip('\n').split('\t')[:2]
    if a != 'alias' and a != k:
        al.setdefault(a, k)   # first row wins (Collins:2011zzd -> Collins2011)
t = open(sys.argv[1], encoding='utf-8').read()
def fix(m):
    keys = [al.get(x.strip(), x.strip()) for x in m.group(2).split(',')]
    return m.group(1) + '{' + ','.join(keys) + '}'
print(re.sub(r'(\\(?:no)?cite(?:\[[^\]]*\])?)\{([^}]*)\}', fix, t), end='')
```

Keys used in agent snippets that are **not** in the merged bib (drop or replace): `Radyushkin1996` (JINR Rapid Commun. 78 (1996) 96; c_ee could not be re-verified here — cite `KrasnikovPivovarov1982` and `KataevStarshenko1995` for the $\pi^2$ terms instead).

**Errata and translations** are in the `note` field (printed). Non-standard fields `inspire`, `erratumdoi` are ignored by BibTeX styles.

**Warnings carried over from the source agents (binding for writers).**

- Collins (2011) uses $D_\mu=\partial_\mu+\ii g A_\mu$; flip $g\to-g$ when copying Wilson-line formulas (survey §0.3). His TMD Fourier transform is $e^{+\ii k_T\cdot b_T}$ (CONTEXT uses $e^{-\ii k_T\cdot b_T}$).
- Qiu–Sterman NPB 378 (1992) 52 (`QiuSterman1992`) is about **direct-photon** SSA; the pion paper is `Qiu:1998ia`.
- BBL use $D^\mu=\partial^\mu+\ii gA^\mu$; their "$M$" in $2\Im f_n/M^{d_n-4}$ is the heavy-quark pole mass (c_scet §6.9).
- Ji PRL 78 (1997) 610 uses $\xi_{\rm Ji}=2\xi$; VGG 1999 use $\varepsilon_{0123}=+1$ (opposite of ours) (c_exclusive §10).
- Becher–Schwartz 2008 App. A prints a spurious $C_F$ in $\Gamma_1$; correct: $\Gamma_1=4C_F[(67/9-\pi^2/3)C_A-20T_Fn_f/9]$ (c_scet §6.10).
- MVV normalisation: $a_s=\alpha_s/4\pi$, $P^{(0)}_{\rm MVV}=2P$, $\gamma^{(0)}_{\rm MVV}=-2\gamma$ (c_pdf §4.4); $c^{(1)}_{2,\rm ns}=2C_2^q$, $c^{(1)}_{2,g}=4n_fC_2^g$ (c_dis §12).
- `SenIndumathiChoudhury2019` is a 2018 arXiv paper on dark matter that reproduces the Grammer–Yennie formulae (its eqs. 7–9); cite it only next to `GrammerYennie1973` as "as reproduced in".
- Rothstein–Stewart's announced "proof of factorization for Drell–Yan with SCET" does not exist as of 2026-10 (survey item 8); do not cite it.

## 4. Classical mathematics

- `Gordan1873` — Gordan, "Ueber die Aufl\osung linearer Gleichungen mit reellen Coefficienten", Math. Ann. 6 (1873) 23. Gordan's theorem of alternatives (used for the momentum-space pinch criterion, App. D, Ch. 3). **Verified:** Crossref DOI 10.1007/BF01442864.
- `Hausdorff1921a` — Hausdorff, "Summationsmethoden und Momentfolgen. I", Math. Z. 9 (1921) 74. Moment problem on a finite interval, part I (uniqueness = easy half; App. K). **Verified:** Crossref DOI 10.1007/BF01378337.
- `Hausdorff1921b` — Hausdorff, "Summationsmethoden und Momentfolgen. II", Math. Z. 9 (1921) 280. Part II. **Verified:** Crossref DOI 10.1007/BF01279032.
- `Hausdorff1923` — Hausdorff, "Momentprobleme f\ur ein endliches Intervall", Math. Z. 16 (1923) 220. Moment problem for a finite interval (App. K). **Verified:** Crossref DOI 10.1007/BF01175684.
- `Szasz1916` — Sz{\'a}sz, "\Uber die Approximation stetiger Funktionen durch lineare Aggregate von Potenzen", Math. Ann. 77 (1916) 482. Müntz–Szász theorem (density of $\{x^{\lambda_k}\}$ iff $\sum1/\lambda_k=\infty$; App. K, cited for completeness). **Verified:** Crossref DOI 10.1007/BF01456964.
- `Muntz1914` — M{\"u}ntz, "\Uber den Approximationssatz von Weierstra\ss", Springer  (1914) 303. Müntz theorem (App. K). **Verified:** Crossref DOI 10.1007/978-3-642-50735-9_22.
- `Carlson1914` — Carlson, "Sur une classe de s\'eries de Taylor", Uppsala University  (1914) . Carlson's theorem (uniqueness of continuation from integers; App. K). Location of the theorem also in `Titchmarsh1939` §5.81 and `Boas1954` Thm 9.2.1 (per c_pdf §9). **Verified:** Open Library record (W. Appelberg, Uppsala 1914).
- `Titchmarsh1939` — Titchmarsh, "The Theory of Functions", Oxford University Press  (1939) . Carlson's theorem, §5.81 (c_pdf §9, location confirmed via Waldschmidt 2020). **Verified:** Open Library (OUP 1939, 2nd ed.).
- `Boas1954` — Boas, "Entire Functions", Academic Press  (1954) . Carlson's theorem, Thm 9.2.1 (c_pdf §9). **Verified:** Open Library (Academic Press 1954).
- `Rudin1987` — Rudin, "Real and Complex Analysis", McGraw-Hill  (1987) . Jensen's formula §§15.16–15.20, Blaschke products §§15.21–15.24, Müntz–Szász §§15.25–15.27 (c_pdf §9). **Verified:** Open Library (McGraw-Hill, 3rd ed. 1987).

## CHANGELOG

**2026-10-10 — spec audit (auditor agent).** Added one entry, `Gardi:2024axt` (copied verbatim from `frontier/others/others.bib`, where it was INSPIRE- and Crossref-verified), for the hidden-region counterexample cited in Ch3. `refs_merged.bib` now has 646 entries; `key_aliases.tsv` gained the identity row. The two frontier bibliographies (`frontier/yzliu/yzliu.bib`, 82 entries; `frontier/others/others.bib`, 238 entries) are otherwise **not** merged: that waits for the lead author's decision on the frontier chapters (FINAL_OUTLINE §0.9). The "645 entries" counts quoted above refer to the version before this change.

Also corrected by the spec audit: the booktitle and year of `Shifman2000` (INSPIRE parent record `Shifman:2001ck`, World Scientific 2001, vol. 3, pp. 1447–1494).
